};

            {detailed.processItems.map((item, index) => (
              <article class="process-card">
                <span class="process-number">
                  {String(index + 1).padStart(2, "0")}
                </span>
                <h3>{item.title}</h3>
                <p>{item.body}</p>
              </article>
            ))}
          </div>
        </section>

        <section class="quality-section">
          <div class="quality-layout section-shell">
            <figure class="quality-visual">
              <img
                src="/about-quality.webp"
                alt={detailed.alts.quality}
                width="1536"
                height="1024"
                loading="lazy"
                decoding="async"
              />
              <figcaption>{detailed.qualityCaption}</figcaption>
            </figure>

            <div class="quality-copy">
              <span class="section-label">{detailed.qualityLabel}</span>
              <h2>{detailed.qualityTitle}</h2>
              <div class="small-line"></div>
              <p>{detailed.qualityBodyOne}</p>
              <p>{detailed.qualityBodyTwo}</p>

              <div class="quality-points">
                {detailed.qualityPoints.map((point) => (
                  <span>{point}</span>
                ))}
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
        </section>

        <section class="shipping-section section-shell">
          <div class="shipping-copy">
            <span class="section-label">{detailed.shippingLabel}</span>
            <h2>{detailed.shippingTitle}</h2>
            <div class="small-line"></div>
            <p>{detailed.shippingBodyOne}</p>
            <p>{detailed.shippingBodyTwo}</p>

            <a
              class="primary-button shipping-button"
              href={whatsappURL}
              target="_blank"
              rel="noopener noreferrer"
              data-whatsapp-context="about-manufacturing"
            >
              {detailed.shippingButton}
            </a>
          </div>

          <figure class="shipping-visual">
            <img
              src="/about-shipping.webp"
              alt={detailed.alts.shipping}
              width="1104"
              height="1472"
              loading="lazy"
              decoding="async"
            />
            <figcaption>{detailed.shippingCaption}</figcaption>
          </figure>
        </section>

        <section class="why-section">
          <div class="section-shell">
            <div class="section-heading why-heading">
              <span class="section-label">{detailed.whyLabel}</span>
              <h2>{detailed.whyTitle}</h2>
            </div>

            <div class="why-grid">
              {detailed.whyItems.map((item) => (
                <article class="why-card">
                  <span class="why-symbol">V</span>
                  <h3>{item.title}</h3>
                  <p>{item.body}</p>
                </article>
              ))}
            </div>
          </div>
        </section>

        <section class="closing-section section-shell">
          <div>
            <span class="section-label">{detailed.closingLabel}</span>
            <h2>{detailed.closingTitle}</h2>
            <p>{detailed.closingBody}</p>
          </div>

          <div class="closing-actions">
            <a
              class="primary-button"
              href={whatsappURL}
              target="_blank"
              rel="noopener noreferrer"
              data-whatsapp-context="about-closing"
            >
              {detailed.whatsapp}
            </a>

            <a class="secondary-button" href={contactURL}>
              {detailed.contactButton}
            </a>
          </div>
        </section>

        <p class="visual-disclosure section-shell">
          {detailed.disclosure}
        </p>
      </div>
    ) : (
      <div class="about-page compact-about">
        <section class="compact-hero">
          <img
            src="/about-factory.webp"
            alt={translations.about.imageAlt}
            width="1536"
            height="1024"
            fetchpriority="high"
            decoding="async"
          />

            <span class="eyebrow">{translations.about.eyebrow}</span>
            <h1>{translations.about.hero}</h1>
            <p>{translations.about.lead}</p>
            <div class="hero-actions">
              <a class="primary-button" href={collectionsURL}>
                {translations.home.button}
              </a>
              <a class="secondary-button" href={contactURL}>
                {translations.contact.button}
              </a>
            </div>
          </div>
        </section>

        <section class="compact-story section-shell">
          <div>
            <span class="section-label">{translations.about.story}</span>
            <h2>{translations.about.heading}</h2>
            <p>{translations.about.paragraphOne}</p>
            <p>{translations.about.paragraphTwo}</p>
          </div>
          <img
            src="/about-design.webp"
            alt={translations.about.imageAlt}
            width="1536"
            height="1024"
            loading="lazy"
            decoding="async"
          />
        </section>

        <section class="compact-expertise section-shell">
          <span class="section-label">{translations.category.eyebrow}</span>
          <h2>{translations.home.heading}</h2>
          <div class="compact-grid">
            <article>
              <h3>{translations.home.livingRooms}</h3>
              <p>{translations.category.livingRooms.description}</p>
            </article>
            <article>
              <h3>{translations.home.diningRooms}</h3>
              <p>{translations.category.diningRooms.description}</p>
            </article>
            <article>
              <h3>{translations.home.bedrooms}</h3>
              <p>{translations.category.bedrooms.description}</p>
            </article>
            <article>
              <h3>{translations.product.worldwideDelivery}</h3>
              <p>{translations.product.worldwideDeliveryText}</p>
            </article>
          </div>
        </section>

        <section class="compact-delivery section-shell">
          <img
            src="/about-shipping.webp"
            alt={translations.contact.imageAlt}
            width="1104"
            height="1472"
            loading="lazy"
            decoding="async"
          />
          <div>
            <span class="section-label">
              {translations.product.worldwideDelivery}
            </span>
            <h2>{translations.contact.heading}</h2>
            <p>{translations.contact.paragraph}</p>
            <a
              class="primary-button"
              href={whatsappURL}
              target="_blank"
              rel="noopener noreferrer"
              data-whatsapp-context="about-localized"
            >
              {translations.contact.button}
            </a>
          </div>
        </section>
      </div>
    )
  }
</BaseLayout>

import BaseLayout from "../layouts/BaseLayout.astro";

import {
  getLanguageFromPath,
  localizedPath,
} from "../i18n/config";

import {
  getManufacturingContent,
} from "../i18n/manufacturing";

const language =
  getLanguageFromPath(Astro.url.pathname);

const content =
  getManufacturingContent(language);

const processImages = [
  "/about-design.webp",
  "/about-carving.webp",
  "/about-upholstery.webp",
  "/about-quality.webp",
] as const;

const processCards =
  content.process.cards.map((card, index) => ({
    ...card,
    image:
      processImages[index] ??
      processImages[0],
  }));

const heroWhatsappURL =
  `https://wa.me/905539478608?text=${encodeURIComponent(
    content.hero.whatsappMessage,
  )}`;

const ctaWhatsappURL =
  `https://wa.me/905539478608?text=${encodeURIComponent(
    content.cta.whatsappMessage,
  )}`;

import BaseLayout from "../layouts/BaseLayout.astro";
import {
  getLanguageFromPath,
  getProductDescription,
  getTranslations,
  localizedPath,
  translatePriceLabel,
  translateProductName,
} from "../i18n/config";

const { product } = Astro.props;

const language = getLanguageFromPath(Astro.url.pathname);
const translations = getTranslations(language);
const translatedProductName = translateProductName(
  product.name,
  language,
);

const categoryTranslationKey = {
  "living-rooms": "livingRooms",
  "dining-rooms": "diningRooms",
  bedrooms: "bedrooms",
  "tv-units": "tvUnits",
} as const;

const categoryKey =
  product.category as keyof typeof categoryTranslationKey;

const categoryTranslation =
  categoryTranslationKey[categoryKey];

const categoryName =
  translations.category[categoryTranslation].title;

const categoryURL = new URL(
  localizedPath(`/${product.category}`, language),
  "https://www.virellaart.com",
).href;

/* =========================
   PRODUCT BASIC DATA
========================= */

const images: string[] = product.images ?? [];

const prices: {
  label: string;
  price: string;
  featured?: boolean;
}[] = product.prices ?? [];

/* =========================
   SEO DATA
========================= */

const pageDescription =
  getProductDescription(product, language);

const canonicalURL = new URL(
  Astro.url.pathname,
  "https://www.virellaart.com",
).href;

const productID = product.name
  .toLowerCase()
  .replaceAll(" ", "-");

const primaryPrice =
  Number(
    prices[0]?.price
      .replace("$", "")
      .replace(",", ""),
  ) || 0;

/* =========================
   PRODUCT SCHEMA
========================= */

const productSchema = {
  "@context": "https://schema.org",
  "@type": "Product",
  name: translatedProductName,
  "@id": `${canonicalURL}#product`,
  url: canonicalURL,
  mainEntityOfPage: canonicalURL,
  sku: productID,
  category: product.category,
  description: pageDescription,
  inLanguage: language,

  brand: {
    "@type": "Brand",
    name: "VIRELLAART",
  },

  image: images.map(
    (image) => `https://www.virellaart.com${image}`,
  ),

  offers: {
    "@type": "Offer",
    url: canonicalURL,
    priceCurrency: "USD",
    price: primaryPrice,
    itemCondition: "https://schema.org/NewCondition",
    availability: "https://schema.org/InStock",

    seller: {
      "@id": "https://www.virellaart.com/#organization",
    },
  },
};

const breadcrumbSchema = {
  "@context": "https://schema.org",
  "@type": "BreadcrumbList",

  itemListElement: [
    {
      "@type": "ListItem",
      position: 1,
      name: translations.nav.home,
      item: new URL(
        localizedPath("/", language),
        "https://www.virellaart.com",
      ).href,
    },
    {
      "@type": "ListItem",
      position: 2,
      name: categoryName,
      item: categoryURL,
    },
    {
      "@type": "ListItem",
      position: 3,
      name: translatedProductName,
      item: canonicalURL,
    },
  ],
};

const productEvent = {
  currency: "USD",
  value: primaryPrice,

  items: [
    {
      item_id: productID,
      item_name: translatedProductName,
      item_brand: "VIRELLAART",
      item_category: categoryName,
      price: primaryPrice,
      quantity: 1,
    },
  ],
};

/* =========================
   WHATSAPP
========================= */

const whatsappNumber = "905539478608";

const whatsappMessage =
  translations.product.whatsappMessage.replace(
    "{product}",
    translatedProductName,
  );

const whatsappUrl =
  `https://wa.me/${whatsappNumber}?text=${encodeURIComponent(
    whatsappMessage,
  )}`;

const productTrust =
  productTrustContent[language] ??
  productTrustContent.en;

    <section class="product-container">

  <div
    class="lightbox"
    id="lightbox"
    role="dialog"
    aria-modal="true"
    aria-hidden="true"
    aria-label={`${translatedProductName} image gallery`}
  >
    <button
      class="lightbox-close"
      id="close"
      type="button"
      aria-label="Close image gallery"
    >
      <span aria-hidden="true">&#215;</span>
    </button>

    {
      images.length > 1 && (
        <button
          class="lightbox-arrow left"
          id="prevImage"
          type="button"
          aria-label={
            translations.product.previousImage
          }
        >
          <span aria-hidden="true">&#8249;</span>
        </button>
      )
    }

    <div class="lightbox-stage" id="lightboxStage">
      <img
        id="lightboxImage"
        src={images[0]}
        alt={translatedProductName}
        width="1600"
        height="1200"
      />
    </div>

    {
      images.length > 1 && (
        <>
          <div
            class="lightbox-counter"
            id="lightboxCounter"
            aria-live="polite"
          >
            1 / {images.length}
          </div>

          <button
            class="lightbox-arrow right"
            id="nextImage"
            type="button"
            aria-label={
              translations.product.nextImage
            }
          >
            <span aria-hidden="true">&#8250;</span>
          </button>
        </>
      )
    }
  </div>

  <script
    is:inline
    define:vars={{
      images,
      translatedProductName,
    }}
  >
    let currentIndex = 0;
    let touchStartX = 0;
    let touchStartY = 0;

    const mainImage =
      document.querySelector("#mainImage");

    const mainImageButton =
      document.querySelector("#mainImageButton");

    const lightbox =
      document.querySelector("#lightbox");

    const lightboxImage =
      document.querySelector("#lightboxImage");

    const lightboxStage =
      document.querySelector("#lightboxStage");

    const lightboxCounter =
      document.querySelector("#lightboxCounter");

    const closeButton =
      document.querySelector("#close");

    const nextButton =
      document.querySelector("#nextImage");

    const previousButton =
      document.querySelector("#prevImage");

    const thumbnails =
      document.querySelectorAll(
        ".thumbnail-button",
      );

    function updateCounter() {
      if (lightboxCounter) {
        lightboxCounter.textContent =
          `${currentIndex + 1} / ${images.length}`;
      }
    }

    function changeImage(index) {
      if (!images.length) {
        return;
      }

      currentIndex =
        (index + images.length) % images.length;

      const selectedImage = images[currentIndex];

      if (mainImage) {
        mainImage.src = selectedImage;
        mainImage.alt =
          `${translatedProductName} ${currentIndex + 1}`;
      }

      if (lightboxImage) {
        lightboxImage.src = selectedImage;
        lightboxImage.alt =
          `${translatedProductName} ${currentIndex + 1}`;
      }

      thumbnails.forEach((item, index2) => {
        item.classList.toggle(
          "active",
          currentIndex === index2,
        );
      });

      updateCounter();
    }

    function showNextImage() {
      changeImage(currentIndex + 1);
    }

    function showPreviousImage() {
      changeImage(currentIndex - 1);
    }

    function openLightbox() {
      if (!lightbox || !images.length) {
        return;
      }

      changeImage(currentIndex);

      lightbox.classList.add("open");
      lightbox.setAttribute(
        "aria-hidden",
        "false",
      );

      document.documentElement.classList.add(
        "lightbox-open",
      );

      closeButton?.focus();
    }

    function closeLightbox() {
      if (!lightbox) {
        return;
      }

      lightbox.classList.remove("open");
      lightbox.setAttribute(
        "aria-hidden",
        "true",
      );

      document.documentElement.classList.remove(
        "lightbox-open",
      );

      mainImageButton?.focus();
    }

    thumbnails.forEach((button) => {
      button.addEventListener("click", () => {
        const index = Number(
          button.getAttribute("data-index"),
        );

        changeImage(index);
      });
    });

    mainImageButton?.addEventListener(
      "click",
      openLightbox,
    );

    closeButton?.addEventListener(
      "click",
      closeLightbox,
    );

    nextButton?.addEventListener(
      "click",
      showNextImage,
    );

    previousButton?.addEventListener(
      "click",
      showPreviousImage,
    );

    lightbox?.addEventListener(
      "click",
      (event) => {
        if (event.target === lightbox) {
          closeLightbox();
        }
      },
    );

    document.addEventListener(
      "keydown",
      (event) => {
        if (
          !lightbox?.classList.contains("open")
        ) {
          return;
        }

        if (event.key === "Escape") {
          event.preventDefault();
          closeLightbox();
        }

        if (event.key === "ArrowRight") {
          event.preventDefault();
          showNextImage();
        }

        if (event.key === "ArrowLeft") {
          event.preventDefault();
          showPreviousImage();
        }
      },
    );

    lightboxStage?.addEventListener(
      "touchstart",
      (event) => {
        const touch = event.changedTouches[0];

        touchStartX = touch.clientX;
        touchStartY = touch.clientY;
      },
      { passive: true },
    );

    lightboxStage?.addEventListener(
      "touchend",
      (event) => {
        const touch = event.changedTouches[0];

        const distanceX =
          touch.clientX - touchStartX;

        const distanceY =
          touch.clientY - touchStartY;

        const isHorizontalSwipe =
          Math.abs(distanceX) >
          Math.abs(distanceY);

        if (
          !isHorizontalSwipe ||
          Math.abs(distanceX) < 45
        ) {
          return;
        }

        if (distanceX < 0) {
          showNextImage();
        } else {
          showPreviousImage();
        }
      },
      { passive: true },
    );
  </script>

      margin-top: 16px;

    .thumbnail-button {

      height: 75px;
      padding: 0;
      flex: 0 0 auto;
      border: 1px solid transparent;
      overflow: hidden;
      background: none;
      cursor: pointer;
    }

    .thumbnail-button img {
      width: 100%;
      height: 100%;
      object-fit: cover;
    }

    .thumbnail-button.active {
      border-color: #c9a55b;
    }

    /* INFORMATION */

    .product-information {
      padding-top: 10px;
    }

    .collection-label {
      margin: 0 0 15px;
      color: #c9a55b;
      font-size: 11px;
      font-weight: 700;

<!DOCTYPE html>
<html lang={language} dir={languages[language].dir}>

<head>
<meta name="p:domain_verify" content="fe900002f59408a3063790cd18fab6eb" />
<meta charset="UTF-8" />

<script
  is:inline
  define:vars={{
    currentLanguage: language,
    supportedLanguages: isEnglishOnlyPage
      ? ["en"]
      : languageCodes,
    englishOnlyPage: isEnglishOnlyPage,
  }}
>
  (() => {
    const storageKey = "virellaart-language";
    const isBot = /bot|crawler|spider|crawling|google|bing|yandex/i.test(
      navigator.userAgent,
    );

    document.addEventListener("click", (event) => {
      const target = event.target;
      const link =
        target instanceof Element
          ? target.closest("[data-language]")
          : null;

      if (link instanceof HTMLElement && link.dataset.language) {
        localStorage.setItem(storageKey, link.dataset.language);
      }
    });

    if (isBot || currentLanguage !== "en" || englishOnlyPage) {
      return;
    }

    const savedLanguage = localStorage.getItem(storageKey);
    const candidates = savedLanguage
      ? [savedLanguage]
      : [...navigator.languages, navigator.language];
    const detectedLanguage = candidates
      .map((candidate) => candidate?.toLowerCase().split("-")[0])
      .find((candidate) => supportedLanguages.includes(candidate));

    if (!detectedLanguage || detectedLanguage === "en") {
      return;
    }

    const path =
      window.location.pathname === "/"
        ? `/${detectedLanguage}/`
        : `/${detectedLanguage}${window.location.pathname}`;

    window.location.replace(
      `${path}${window.location.search}${window.location.hash}`,
    );
  })();
</script>

<!-- Google Analytics -->

<script is:inline>
document.addEventListener("click", (event) => {
  const target = event.target;
  const button =
    target instanceof Element
      ? target.closest('a[href*="wa.me"], a[href*="api.whatsapp.com"]')
      : null;

  if (!(button instanceof HTMLAnchorElement)) return;

  if (typeof window.gtag === "function") {
    const eventData = {
      cta_position: button.dataset.whatsappContext || "page",
      product_name: button.dataset.product || undefined,
      page_language: document.documentElement.lang,
      link_url: button.href,
      transport_type: "beacon"
    };

    window.gtag("event", "whatsapp_click", {
      event_category: "engagement",
      event_label: "WhatsApp Button",
      ...eventData
    });

    window.gtag("event", "generate_lead", {
      method: "WhatsApp",
      ...eventData
    });
  }
});
</script>

  seo: {

  };

    highlight: string;

    paragraphs: string[];

      label: string;
      value: string;
    }[];

  language: string,
